RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 3.766e+06 area_delay_product  (eval 17)

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='starting_point.v', limit=400, offset=646)

[Step 4/60]

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

//                             V0.1

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096

// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

... (showing lines 1-147 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 7/60]
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+5], coeffs[b*64+r*8+7]);
        end
      end
      // inter-block gap: >= 2 idle cycles (serializer IDLE+SETUP), varied a bit
      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end
    drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    repeat (400) @(negedge clk);  // flush x + transpose + y pipeline

    if (out_cnt != num_blocks * 64) begin
      $display("TB_ERROR missing outputs: got %0d, expected %0d", out_cnt, num_blocks * 64);
      total_errors = total_errors + 1;
    end
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end

Now let me write the full optimized design. I'll build a round-based 1-D engine with 7 fixed-constant multipliers (much cheaper than the original's 10 general 32×32 multipliers).


  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content='//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  — 8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG), bit-exact rewrite of\n// ultraembedded/core_jpeg jpeg_idct_x -> transpose -> jpeg_idct_y.\n//\n// Key differences vs. the reference RTL (functionally identical):\n//  * every multiply is by a *constant* (cos*4096) - the 7 fixed\n//    multipliers are shared between two "rounds" instead of the\n//    reference\'s 5 fully general 32x32 multipliers per pass.\n//  * the double-buffered 4x32x32 corner-turn RAM is replaced by two\n//    64x20 transpose banks written 8 values at a time by the row pass\n//    and read as an 8-value bit slice by the column pass.\n//  * narrower datapaths (row pass 31b, column pass 32b to keep the\n//    reference\'s 32-bit wrap-around semantics).\n//  * the 1/sqrt(2) scaling (x*181)/256 is done as a shift/add tree.\n//\n// Arithmetic (per 1-D pass), identical to the reference:\n//   s0=(x0+x4)*C4 s1=(x0-x4)*C4 s2=x2*C6-x6*C2 s3=x2*C2+x6*C6\n//   s4=x0*C7-x6*C1 s5=x4*C3-x2*C5 s6=x4*C5+x2*C3 s7=x0*C1+x6*C7\n//   t0=s0+s3 t3=s0-s3 t1=s1+s2 t2=s1-s2 t7=s6+s7 t6=s7-s6 t4=s4+s5 t5=s4-s5\n//   q5=(t6-t5)*181/256  q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4 w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct1d #(\n    parameter integer IW = 16,   // input width  (signed)\n    parameter integer DW = 31,   // datapath width (signed)\n    parameter integer SH = 11,   // output arithmetic shift\n    parameter integer PS = 1     // phase at which multiply round A runs\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire [2:0]           ph,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x0, x1, x2, x3,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x4, x5, x6, x7,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w0, w1, w2, w3,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w4, w5, w6, w7\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1 = 13\'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2 = 13\'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3 = 13\'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4 = 13\'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5 = 13\'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6 = 13\'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7 = 13\'sd 799;\n\nlocalparam [2:0] PA = PS[2:0];\nlocalparam [2:0] PB = (PS + 1) & 7;\nlocalparam [2:0] PC = (PS + 2) & 7;\nlocalparam [2:0] PD = (PS + 3) & 7;\nlocalparam [2:0] PE = (PS + 4) & 7;\nlocalparam [2:0] PF = (PS + 5) & 7;\n\n// ---- multiplier operand select (round A / round B) ----------------------\nwire selb = (ph == PB);\n\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m0 = selb ? x4 : x0;  // * C4\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m1 = selb ? x6 : x0;  // * C1\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m2 = selb ? x6 : x2;  // * C2\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m3 = selb ? x4 : x2;  // * C3\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m4 = selb ? x2 : x4;  // * C5\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m5 = selb ? x2 : x6;  // * C6\nwire signed [IW-1:0] m6 = selb ? x0 : x6;  // * C7\n\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p0 = m0 * C4;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p1 = m1 * C1;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p2 = m2 * C2;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p3 = m3 * C3;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p4 = m4 * C5;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p5 = m5 * C6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] p6 = m6 * C7;\n\nreg signed [DW-1:0] A0,A1,A2,A3,A4,A5,A6;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] B0,B1,B2,B3,B4,B5,B6;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] s0,s1,s2,s3,s4,s5,s6,s7;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] t0,t1,t2,t3,t4,t5,t6,t7;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] q5,q6;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] r0,r1,r2,r3,r4,r5,r6,r7;\n\n// (v*181)/256  ==  (v>>>8)+(v>>>5)+(v>>>3)+(v>>>1)\nwire signed [DW-1:0] d5 = (t6 - t5);\nwire signed [DW-1:0] d6 = (t5 + t6);\nwire signed [DW-1:0] e5 = (d5 >>> 8) + (d5 >>> 5) + (d5 >>> 3) + (d5 >>> 1);\nwire signed [DW-1:0] e6 = (d6 >>> 8) + (d6 >>> 5) + (d6 >>> 3) + (d6 >>> 1);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    case (ph)\n    PA: begin\n        A0 <= p0; A1 <= p1; A2 <= p2; A3 <= p3;\n        A4 <= p4; A5 <= p5; A6 <= p6;\n    end\n    PB: begin\n        B0 <= p0; B1 <= p1; B2 <= p2; B3 <= p3;\n        B4 <= p4; B5 <= p5; B6 <= p6;\n        s3 <= A2 + A5;   // x2*C2 + x6*C6\n        s6 <= A4 + A3;   // x4*C5 + x2*C3\n        s7 <= A1 + A6;   // x0*C1 + x6*C7\n    end\n    PC: begin\n        s0 <= A0 + B0;   // (x0+x4)*C4\n        s1 <= A0 - B0;   // (x0-x4)*C4\n        s2 <= B5 - A2;   // x2*C6 - x6*C2\n        s4 <= B6 - B1;   // x0*C7 - x6*C1\n        s5 <= B3 - B4;   // x4*C3 - x2*C5\n    end\n    PD: begin\n        t0 <= s0 + s3; t3 <= s0 - s3;\n        t1 <= s1 + s2; t2 <= s1 - s2;\n        t7 <= s6 + s7; t6 <= s7 - s6;\n        t4 <= s4 + s5; t5 <= s4 - s5;\n    end\n    PE: begin\n        q5 <= e5; q6 <= e6;\n        r0 <= (t0 + t7) >>> SH;\n        r7 <= (t0 - t7) >>> SH;\n        r3 <= (t3 + t4) >>> SH;\n        r4 <= (t3 - t4) >>> SH;\n    end\n    PF: begin\n        r2 <= (t2 + q5) >>> SH;\n        r5 <= (t2 - q5) >>> SH;\n        r1 <= (t1 + q6) >>> SH;\n        r6 <= (t1 - q6) >>> SH;\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nassign w0 = r0;\nassign w1 = r1;\nassign w2 = r2;\nassign w3 = r3;\nassign w4 = r4;\nassign w5 = r5;\nassign w6 = r6;\nassign w7 = r7;\n\nendmodule\n')

results are registered 7 cycles later.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct1d #(\n    parameter integer IW = 16,   // input width  (signed)\n    parameter integer PW = 28,   // product width (signed, exact)\n    parameter integer DW = 32,   // datapath width (signed, wrapping)\n    parameter integer SH = 11    // output arithmetic shift\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire                 go,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x0, x1, x2, x3,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x4, x5, x6, x7,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w0, w1, w2, w3,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w4, w5, w6, w7,\n    output wire                 ovalid\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K1 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K2 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K3 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K4 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K5 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K6 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] K7 = 13'sd 799;\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Phase counter: 0 = idle, 1..7 = pipeline stages\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [2:0] ph;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (go)          ph <= 3'd1;\n    else if (ph != 3'd0) ph <= ph + 3'd1;\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Multiplier operand selects (round A at ph==1, round B at ph==2)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire       ra = (ph == 3'd1);\n\nwire signed [IW-1:0] o0 = ra ? x0 : x4;   // * K4\nwire signed [IW-1:0] o1 = ra ? x2 : x6;   // * K6\nwire signed [IW-1:0] o2 = ra ? x2 : x6;   // * K2\nwire signed [IW-1:0] o3 = ra ? x1 : x7;   // * K7\nwire signed [IW-1:0] o4 = ra ? x5 : x3; end\nendfunction\n\n// (v*181)/256 with 32-bit wrapping multiply, truncation toward zero\nwire signed [DW-1:0] dv5 = t6 - t5;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] dv6 = t5 + t6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] m5  = (dv5 <<< 7) + (dv5 <<< 5) + (dv5 <<< 4) + (dv5 <<< 2) + dv5;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] m6  = (dv6 <<< 7) + (dv6 <<< 5) + (dv6 <<< 4) + (dv6 <<< 2) + dv6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] n5  = (m5 + (m5[DW-1] ? {{(DW-8){1'b0}}, 8'd255} : {DW{1'b0}})) >>> 8;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] n6  = (m6 + (m6[DW-1] ? {{(DW-8){1'b0}}, 8'd255} : {DW{1'b0}})) >>> 8;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    case (ph)\n    3'd1: begin\n        A0 <= p0; I found the bug: in the odd-part products, both rounds use the same operands, so I only need 6 fixed multipliers plus one time-shared C4 multiplier. Let me rewrite correctly.

Now I'll write the final optimized design with two pipelined constant-multiplier engines and a banked transpose.


  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  —  8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n//\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the ultraembedded/core_jpeg\n// jpeg_idct_x -> jpeg_idct_transpose -> jpeg_idct_y composition.\n//\n// 1-D core (identical in both passes, only the shift differs):\n//   s0=(x0+x4)*C4    s1=(x0-x4)*C4\n//   s2=x2*C6-x6*C2   s3=x2*C2+x6*C6\n//   s4=x1*C7-x7*C1   s5=x5*C3-x3*C5\n//   s6=x5*C5+x3*C3   s7=x1*C1+x7*C7\n//   t0=s0+s3 t3=s0-s3 t1=s1+s2 t2=s1-s2\n//   t7=s6+s7 t6=s7-s6 t4=s4+s5 t5=s4-s5\n//   q5=(t6-t5)*181/256   q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4\n//   w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//\n// Optimisations vs. the reference RTL:\n//  * all 14 products are formed by 7 *constant* multipliers, each used for\n//    exactly two operand rounds (the reference uses 5 fully general 32x32\n//    multipliers per pass, i.e. 10 in total)\n//  * the 4x32x32 double-buffered corner-turn RAM is replaced by two\n//    8x8x20 transpose sets written a whole row per cycle (shared address,\n//    so the write path needs no mux at all) and read four at a time\n//  * the row pass keeps exact 20-bit results (proved bound |w| <= 8.1e8\n//    gives |w>>>11| <= 395254), the column pass keeps the reference's\n//    32-bit wrap-around semantics\n//  * (x*181)/256 is a 32-bit wrapping shift/add tree with the reference's\n//    truncate-toward-zero division\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_eng #(\n    parameter integer IW  = 16,   // signed input width\n    parameter integer SH  = 11,   // output arithmetic shift\n    parameter integer PH0 = 1     // phase of the round-A product capture\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire [2:0]           ph,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x0, x1, x2, x3,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x4, x5, x6, x7,\n    output wire signed [31:0] PA = PH0[2:0];              // round A\nlocalparam [2:0] PB = (PH0 + 1) & 7;         // round B\nlocalparam [2:0] PC = (PH0 + 2) & 7;         // t\nlocalparam [2:0] PD = (PH0 + 3) & 7;         // q\nlocalparam [2:0] PE = (PH0 + 4) & 7;         // w\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// multiplier operand / constant selection\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nwire rb = (ph == PB);\n\nwire signed [IW:0] sx0  = {x0[IW-1], x0};\nwire signed [IW:0] sx4  = {x4[IW-1], x4};\nwire signed [IW:0] sum4 = sx0 + sx4;\nwire signed [IW:0] dif4 = sx0 - sx4;\nwire signed [IW:0] op4  = rb ? dif4 : sum4;\n\nwire signed [IW-1:0] oC6 = rb ? x6 : x2;\nwire signed [IW-1:0] oC2 = rb ? x2 : x6;\nwire signed [IW-1:0] oC7 = rb ? x7 : x1;\nwire signed [IW-1:0] oC1 = rb ? x1 : x7;\nwire signed [IW-1:0] oC3 = rb ? x3 : x5;\nwire signed [IW-1:0] oC5 = rb ? x5 : x3;\n\nwire signed [31:0] p4  = Correctness passes but delay regressed badly — the transpose read mux fanout is the bottleneck (a flop driving ~320 mux gates). Let me fix that with a registered one-hot AND-OR read.

  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  —  8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n//\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the q5=(t6-t5)*181/256   q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4\n//   w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//\n// Optimisations vs. the reference RTL:\n//  * all replaced by a 16x8x20\n//    register block whose read uses a registered one-hot AND-OR select\n//    (a binary mux makes one flop drive hundreds of gates -> huge delay)\n//  * (x*181)/256 is a 32-bit .w4(yw4), .w5(yw5), .w6(yw6), .w7(yw7)\n);\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// output buffer (one column) + The operand mux in the multiply path is the bottleneck. Let me eliminate it entirely by using phase-enabled operand registers (no datapath mux).


  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  —  8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n//\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the ultraembedded/core_jpeg\n// jpeg_idct_x -> jpeg_idct_transpose -> jpeg_idct_y composition.\n//\n// 1-D core (identical in both passes, only the shift differs):\n//   s0=(x0+x4)*C4    s1=(x0-x4)*C4\n//   s2=x2*C6-x6*C2   s3=x2*C2+x6*C6\n//   s4=x1*C7-x7*C1   s5=x5*C3-x3*C5\n//   s6=x5*C5+x3*C3   s7=x1*C1+x7*C7\n//   t0=s0+s3 t3=s0-s3 t1=s1+s2 t2=s1-s2\n//   t7=s6+s7 t6=s7-s6 t4=s4+s5 t5=s4-s5\n//   q5=(t6-t5)*181/256   q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4\n//   w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//\n// 1-D core pipeline (phase 1..6, results registered at phase 6):\n//   1: latch round-A operands      2: round-A products, latch B operands\n//   3: round-B products            4: t values\n//   5: q values                    6: w values\n// The operand round select is done with register *enables* (phase decode),\n// never with a datapath mux, so no multiply path contains a mux.\n//\n// Optimisations vs. the reference RTL:\n//  * 7 constant multipliers per engine instead of five general 32x32 ones\n//    per pass (ten in total)\n//  * the 4x32x32 double-buffered corner-turn RAM is replaced by a 16x8x20\n//    register block; a column is read out with a registered one-hot AND-OR\n//    select (a binary mux would make one flop drive hundreds of gates)\n//  * (x*181)/256 is a 32-bit wrapping shift/add tree with the reference's\n//    truncate-toward-zero division\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_eng #(\n    parameter integer IW = 16,   // signed input width\n    parameter integer SH = 11    // output arithmetic shift\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire [2:0]           ph,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x0, x1, x2, x3,\n    input  wire mux)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg signed [IW:0]   oa4, ob4;     // (x0+x4), (x0-x4)\nreg signed [IW-1:0] oa6, oa2, oa7, oa1, oa3, oa5;\nreg signed [IW-1:0] ob6, ob2, ob7, ob1, ob3, ob5;\n\nwire signed [IW:0] sum4 = {x0[IW-1], x0} + {x4[IW-1], x4};\nwire signed [IW:0] dif4 = {x0[IW-1], x0} - {x4[IW-1], x4};\n\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pa4 = oa4 * C4;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pa6 = oa6 * C6;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pa2 = oa2 * C2;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pa7 = oa7 * C7;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pa1 = oa1 * C1;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pa3 = oa3 * C3;\nwire signed pb1 = ob1 * C1;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pb3 = ob3 * C3;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] pb5 = ob5 * C5;\n\n// shared multiplier: round A at phase 2, round B at phase 3\nreg signed [PW-1:0] A4, A6, A2, A7, A1, A3, level\n//=================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n     input  wire        clk\n    ,input  wire        rst\n    ,input  wire        img_start_i\n   -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  —  8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n//\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the q5=(t6-t5)*181/256   q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4\n//   w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//\n// Optimisations vs. the reference RTL:\n//  * 9 [ASSISTANT]
The 32-bit `t7→q6` chain is too long. Let me restructure the pipeline into 7 short stages (products, 2-level t-tree, dv, q, w) with w stored directly into the transpose.


  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  —  8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n//\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the ultraembedded/core_jpeg\n// jpeg_idct_x -> jpeg_idct_transpose -> jpeg_idct_y composition.\n//\n// 1-D core (identical in both passes, only the widths/shift differ):\n//   s0=(x0+x4)*C4    s1=(x0-x4)*C4\n//   s2=x2*C6-x6*C2   s3=x2*C2+x6*C6\n//   s4=x1*C7-x7*C1   s5=x5*C3-x3*C5\n//   s6=x5*C5+x3*C3   s7=x1*C1+x7*C7\n//   t0=s0+s3 t3=s0-s3 t1=s1+s2 t2=s1-s2\n//   t7=s6+s7 t6=s7-s6 t4=s4+s5 t5=s4-s5\n//   q5=(t6-t5)*181/256   q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4\n//   w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//\n// Pipeline (one register stage per cycle, so each stage is at most a\n// constant multiply or a single adder level):\n//   ph1 latch round-A operands        ph2 round-A products, latch B ops\n//   ph3 round-B products              ph4 t values  (2-level product tree)\n//   ph5 dv=t6-t5, t5+t6               ph6 q values\n//   ph7 w values (written straight into the destination register file)\n//\n// Optimisations vs. the reference RTL:\n//  * every multiply is by a *constant* (C1..C7, plus 181 for the 1/sqrt(2)\n//    scale) instead of five fully general 32x32 multipliers per pass\n//  * the round select uses register enables, never a datapath mux\n//  * the 4x32x32 double-buffered corner-turn RAM is replaced by a 16x8x20\n//    register file; a column is read with a registered one-hot AND-OR\n//    select (a binary mux makes one flop drive hundreds of gates)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_eng #(\n    parameter integer IW = 16,   // signed input width\n    parameter integer DW = 31,   // datapath width (signed, wrapping)\n    parameter integer SH = 11    // output arithmetic shift\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire [2:0]           ph,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x0, x1, x2, x3,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x4, x5, x6, x7,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w0, w1, w2, w3,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w4, w5, w6, w7\n);\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7 = 13'sd 799;\n\nlocalparam integer PW = IW + 13;     // exact product width\n\n//---- round operands : one register set, reloaded per round -------------\nreg signed [IW:0]   om4;\nreg signed [IW-1:0] om6, om2, om7, om1, om3, om5;\n\nwire signed [IW:0] sum4 = {x0[IW-1], x0} + {x4[IW-1], x4};\nwire signed [IW:0] dif4 = {x0[IW-1], x0} - {x4[IW-1], x4};\n\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p4 = om4 * C4;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p6 = om6 * C6;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p2 = om2 * C2;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p7 = om7 * C7;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p1 = om1 * C1;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p3 = om3 * C3;\nwire signed [PW-1:0] p5 = om5 * C5;\n\nreg signed [PW-1:0] A4, A6, A2, A7, A1, A3, A5;\nreg signed [PW-1:0] B4, B6, B2, B7, B1, B3, B5;\n\nreg signed [DW-1:0] t0, t1, t2, t3, t4, t5, t6, t7;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] dv5, dv6;\nreg signed [DW-1:0] q5, q6;\n\nfunction signed [DW-1:0] ext;\n    input signed [PW-1:0] v;\n    begin ext = {{(DW-PW){v[PW-1]}}, v}; end\nendfunction\n\n// t values : two-level tree straight from the product registers\nwire signed [DW-1:0] a3b6 = ext(B2) + ext(B6);   // s3\nwire signed [DW-1:0] a2b6 = ext(A6) - ext(A2);   // s2\nwire signed [DW-1:0] a7a1 = ext(A7) - ext(A1);   // s4\nwire signed [DW-1:0] a3a5 = ext(A3) - ext(A5);   // s5\nwire signed [DW-1:0] b5b3 = ext(B5) + ext(B3);   // s6\nwire signed [DW-1:0] b1b7 = ext(B1) + ext(B7);   // s7\n\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt0 = ext(A4) + a3b6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt3 = ext(A4) - a3b6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt1 = ext(B4) + a2b6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt2 = ext(B4) - a2b6;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt7 = b5b3 + b1b7;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt6 = b1b7 - b5b3;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt4 = a7a1 + a3a5;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nt5 = a7a1 - a3a5;\n\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nd5 = t6 - t5;\nwire signed [DW-1:0] nd6 = t5 + t6;\n\n// 1/sqrt(2) : (v*181)/256, 32-bit wrapping, division truncating toward zero\nfunction signed [DW-1:0] sc181;\n    input signed [DW-1:0] v;\n    reg   signed [DW-1:0] p;\n    reg   signed [DW+7:0] pe;\n    begin\n        p  = v * 13'sd181;\n        pe = {{8{p[DW-1]}}, p};\n        if (p[DW-1]) pe = pe + 40'sd255;\n        sc181 = pe >>> 8;\n    end\nendfunction\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    case (ph)\n    3'd1: begin\n        om4 <= sum4; om6 <= x2; om2 <= x6;\n        om7 <= x1;   om1 <= x7; om3 <= x5; om5 <= x3;\n    end\n    3'd2: begin\n        om4 <= dif4; om6 <= x6; om2 <= x2;\n        om7 <= x7;   om1 <= x1; om3 <= x3; om5 <= x5;\n        A4 <= p4; A6 <= p6; A2 <= p2; A7 <= p7;\n sc181(dv5);\n        q6 <= sc181(dv6);\n    end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nassign w0 = (t0 + t7) >>> SH;\nassign w7 = (t0 - t7) >>> SH;\nassign w3 = (t3 + t4) >>> SH;\nassign w4 = (t3 - t4) SH;\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//=================================================================\n// top level\n//=================================================================\nmodule jpeg_idct_2d\n(\n (inport_idx_i == 3'd0);\nwire beat1 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd1);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (beat0) begin\n        rx0 <= inport_data0_i;\n        rx2 <= inport_data1_i;\n        rx4 <= inport_data2_i;\n        rx6 <= inport_data3_i;\n    end\n    if (beat1) begin\n        rx1 <= inport_data0_i;\n        rx3 <= inport_data1_i;\n        rx5 <= inport_data2_i;\n        rx7 <= inport_data3_i;\n    end\nend\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// row pass : 8-cycle period per input row, w stored at phase 7\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nreg [2:0] rph;\nreg [2:0] rcnt;\nreg [2:0] wrow;\nreg       wb;\nreg       ready0, ready1;\nreg       xact;\n\nwire xwr = (rph == 3'd7) && xact;\n\nwire signed [30:0] rw0, rw1, rw2, rw3, rw4, rw5, rw6, rw7;\n\nidct_eng #(.IW(16), .DW(31), .SH(11)) u_row\n(\n    .clk(clk), .ph(rph),\n    .x0(rx0), .x1(rx1), .x2(rx2), == 3'd7) soh <= 16'd1 << {rb2, lnext};\nend\n\nwire [19:0] rdv [0:7];\n\ngenvar gk, gi;\ngenerate\nfor (gk = 0; gk < 8; gk = gk + 1) begin : g_rd\n    wire [19:0] m0 [0:7];\n    wire [19:0] m1 Now let me replace the constant multipliers with CSD (canonical signed digit) shift-add trees — much shorter depth than Yosys's array multiplier.




  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d  —  8x8 2-D IDCT (baseline JPEG)\n//\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the ultraembedded/core_jpeg\n// jpeg_idct_x -> jpeg_idct_transpose -> jpeg_idct_y composition.\n//\n// 1-D core (identical in both passes, only the widths/shift differ):\n//   s0=(x0+x4)*C4    s1=(x0-x4)*C4\n//   s2=x2*C6-x6*C2   s3=x2*C2+x6*C6\n//   s4=x1*C7-x7*C1   s5=x5*C3-x3*C5\n//   s6=x5*C5+x3*C3   s7=x1*C1+x7*C7\n//   t0=s0+s3 t3=s0-s3 t1=s1+s2 t2=s1-s2\n//   t7=s6+s7 t6=s7-s6 t4=s4+s5 t5=s4-s5\n//   q5=(t6-t5)*181/256   q6=(t5+t6)*181/256\n//   w0=t0+t7 w7=t0-t7 w3=t3+t4 w4=t3-t4\n//   w2=t2+q5 w5=t2-q5 w1=t1+q6 w6=t1-q6\n//   out = w >>> SH\n//\n// Implementation notes\n//  * the cos constants are applied as CSD (canonical signed digit)\n//    shift/add trees - each is only 4-6 signed digits, so the multiply\n//    is 2-3 adder levels instead of an array multiplier\n//  * the 1/sqrt(2) scale (x*181)/256 is also a shift/add tree; because\n//    everything is evaluated modulo 2^32 this is exactly the reference's\n//    wrapping multiply followed by the truncating division\n//  * the round select uses register enables, never a datapath mux\n//  * the 4x32x32 double-buffered corner-turn RAM is replaced by a 16x8x20\n//    register file; a column is read with a registered one-hot AND-OR\n//    select (a binary mux makes one flop drive hundreds of gates)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_eng #(\n    parameter integer IW = 16,   // signed input width\n    parameter integer DW = 31,   // datapath width (signed, wrapping)\n    parameter integer SH = 11    // output arithmetic shift\n)(\n    input  wire                 clk,\n    input  wire [2:0]           ph,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x0, x1, x2, x3,\n    input  wire signed [IW-1:0] x4, x5, x6, x7,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w0, w1, w2, w3,\n    output wire signed [DW-1:0] w4, w5, w6, w7\n);\n\nlocalparam integer PW = IW + 14;     // full product width\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// CSD shift/add multiply by a constant (32-bit wrapping)\n//   C1 = 2^12 - 2^6  - 2^4  + 2^0\n//   C2 = 2^12 - 2^8  - 2^6  + 2^3\n//   C3 = 2^12 - 2^10 + 2^8  + 2^6  + 2^4  - 2^1\n//   C4 = 2^12 - 2^10 - 2^8  + 2^6  + 2^4\n//   C5 = 2^12 - 2^11 + 2^8  - 2^5  + 2^2\n//   C6 = 2^12 - 2^11 - 2^9  + 2^5  - 2^0\n//   C7 = 2^10 - 2^8  + 2^5  - 2^0\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nfunction signed [31:0] mul_c1;\n    input signed [IW-1:0] xi;\n    reg   signed [31:0] x;\n    begin\n        x = xi;\n        mul_c1 = ((x <<< 12) - (x <<< 6)) - ((x <<< 4) - x);\n    end\nendfunction\n\nfunction signed [31:0] mul_c2;\n    input signed [IW-1:0] xi;\n    reg   signed [31:0] x;\n    begin\n        x = xi;\n [ASSISTANT]
## Summary

### 1. Approaches tried, ranked by outcome

| Version | Approach | ADP | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| `starting_point.v` | Original core_jpeg (5 general 32×32 mults/pass, 4×32×32 dual-port transpose RAM) | 9.50e6 | baseline |
| `design.sv` (final PASS) | 2 constant-multiplier engines + 16×8×20 register-file corner turn + 7-stage pipeline | **3.77e6** | **best (2.52× better)** |
| intermediate | 7 constant mults, one register set, shared transpose | 3.92e6 | PASS |
| intermediate | 14 separate A/B multiplier instances (mistake) | 6.27e6 | PASS, slower |
| intermediate | mux-based operand select in multiply path | 5.44e6 / 6.83e6 | PASS, slower |

### 2. Optimizations with the most impact

1. **Replacing general 32×32 multipliers with constant multiplies** — the single biggest win. `y*C` and `x*C4` (10 general mults) became 7 constants per engine (C1..C7 + 181).
2. **Register-file corner turn instead of a dual-port RAM array** — the original 4×32×32 RAM's binary read mux made *one flop drive ~320 gates* (1447 ps just leaving the flop). Two fixes mattered:
   - Transpose layout `tr[{bank,col}][row]` so a whole **column is read as one contiguous slice** (8 separate reads, row index constant per output).
   - **Registered one-hot AND-OR select** instead of a binary mux → no high-fanout flop.
3. **Pipelining into short stages** — the decisive structural change. Products → 2-level t-tree → dv → ×181 → w. Every stage is at most one constant multiply or one adder level.
4. **Register-enable operand select instead of a datapath mux** — keeps the phase decode out of the multiply path.
5. **Narrowing the row pass to 31 bits / 20-bit transpose storage** — provable bound `|w>>>11| ≤ 395254` fits in 20 bits.

### 3. What didn't work / caused regressions

- **Phase-decode → mux → multiply** chain: the `selb` flop drove the mux, adding 570 ps. Replaced with enable-controlled operand registers.
- **Instantiated 14 multipliers** instead of 7 by having separate A/B operand registers — nearly doubled area. Fixed by one operand register set reloaded per round.
- **`*181/256` as a shift/add tree in one stage** — 32-bit carry chain, 1328 ps critical path. Split into multiply stage + truncating-shift stage.
- **`ext()` zero-extension when PW > 32** — silently dropped the sign bit; had to use implicit resize to get 32-bit wraparound.
- **CSD decompositions written from memory** were wrong for C1/C2/C5/C6/C7 (e.g. C6 = 1567 = 2^11 − 2^9 + 2^5 − 1, not what I first wrote). This was the last fix applied.
- **`PW = IW + 13`** was one bit short → 4799 errors.

### 4. Lessons learned

- **In this flow, fanout is the delay killer, not logic depth.** A "nice" register-file with a binary mux read produces a flop with 300+ loads and dominates everything. One-hot read or banked reads are essential.
- **Always keep the multiply path mux-free.** Register enables are free; a mux in front of a multiplier is not.
- **Constant multipliers (CSD shift/add) beat inferred `*` in Yosys** — but only if the decomposition is verified numerically. I should have written the CSD check *before* editing RTL.
- **Verify fixed-point semantics bit-exactly**: 32-bit wraparound must apply to the `*181` multiply even in the narrower row pass — the row pass is 31-bit but the reference's scaling still wraps at 32.
- **Next time**: (a) build the transpose + one-hot read first and confirm correctness, then add the engines; (b) use a `$display`-based self-check on constants; (c) the remaining 941 ps critical path (still `om→A` = CSD multiply) could likely be shortened by splitting the CSD tree across two phases, and `target_delay` was never swept — trying ~700–900 ps would probably have helped further.
